- **Phase:** 179.1 — Review Fixes: Access, Database Rules and Contestant App Behaviour
- **Opened by:** `179.1-01`, 2026-10-25
- **Read by:** `node .planning/phases/179.1-review-fixes-access-database-rules-contestant-app/179.1-verdict.mjs ledger [--closed]`

**The rule (execution rules § The negative-control ledger).** Every new or rewritten test of the phase has a row,
written by the plan that adds it before that plan's close: its id (`<plan>-<n>`), the plan, the test (file and
title), what "without its change" was (a revert, the tree before the commit, or an inverted expectation), the
command and the run or log that showed it red with its first failing line pasted, and the run or log that showed it
green. A row whose red was not observed keeps `pending` in its outcome cell and is never written as confirmed.

The table below is the register: one table, one row per test, no row spanning lines. A `|` inside a cell is written
`\|`.

| id | plan | test | without its change | red (command, run or log, first failing line) | green (run or log) | outcome |

| 88-1 | 179.1-88 | `apps/frontend/src/lib/auth/logoutAnnouncement.test.ts` › `the announcement of a Log out between tabs` › rewritten for the start and end pair, 15 cases. New: `is made on the channel named openvaa-logout, a start counting for 30 000 ms and an end for 5 000 ms` (rewritten from `is made on the channel named openvaa-logout, and is heard for 5 000 ms`); `still counts a start 8 000 ms later while no end has come, as a slow Log out`; `counts a start with no end at 30 000 ms and not at 30 001 ms`; `counts an end with no start before it at 5 000 ms and not at 5 001 ms` (in place of `counts as heard at 5 000 ms after the announcement and not at 5 001 ms`); `counts nothing 5 001 ms after an end, though the start it closed is under 30 000 ms old`; `does not hear a message of another value on the channel`; `counts nothing when the clock is set back before a heard start or a heard end`. Carried from plan 83 on the call form `announceLogout('start')` / `('end')`, expectations unchanged: `hears a start another document announces` (from `is heard by a document that listens when another document announces`), `is not heard by the document that makes it, while another document hears it`, `leaves no channel open in a document that announces without listening, and is still delivered`, `keeps one channel per document however many listen, and closes it when the last one stops`, `is not heard by a document that has stopped listening`, and the three cases of `BroadcastChannel` undefined or throwing; their red is row `83-1` | the slow Log out, the cap, the other word and the constants: the tree before the change, the cases committed alone (`4fe4ffd29`) before `d6456b79c`.
